HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_259527d19fd45eb9ac69","text":"2. **Fear as knowing.** Lexical evidence: خشي B002 knowing as figurative use; علم B001 knowing in 87:7; جهر/خفى open-hidden knowledge. Structural cue: divine knowledge precedes human خشية. Assigned role: fear may include recognition of what is known/revealed. Abductive move: the packet supplies the knowledge-branch of خشية; I infer that the fearful person \"remembers\" because he comes to know, not merely because he feels dread. Reading change: the ayah reads as epistemic reverence: knowledge becomes awe and recall.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000413/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_270ad9aaff813faf4bef","text":"3. **Dryness warning.** Lexical evidence: خ ش ي B004 (shriveling/dryness), غ ث و B002 in 87:5 (dried pasture chaff), ح ي ي B002 in 87:13/16 (life by rain). Structural cue: dried chaff precedes the reminder-response scene. Assigned role: khashya carries a bodily/ecological warning of drying out if reminder is not received. Abductive move: the hearer's inner state can mirror the pasture's drying. Reading change: remembering becomes a way not to become spiritually chaff-like.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_290cee94e51a394805ec","text":"1. **Reverent fear as the receptor of reminder.** Lexical evidence: ذ ك ر B003/B009 (recalling/admonition), خ ش ي B001 (fear, dread, reverent awe). Structural cue: 87:9's conditional benefit is answered by the subject who fears. Assigned role: khashya is the affective condition that lets reminder become actual recollection. Abductive move: admonition requires a receptive interior disposition. Reading change: fear is not merely emotion but the cognitive aperture through which remembrance activates.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000413/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_38acd6080eee589af0f2","text":"2. **Awe is cognitively informed.** `خ ش ي/B001` supplies fear or reverent awe, while dormant `خ ش ي/B002` supplies knowing in figurative use. The explicit `يَعْلَمُ` of 87:7 and the split between recollection and avoidance in 87:10–11 make discernment the structural activator. Knowledge gives awe an apprehended object and thereby makes the hearer receptive to reminder. I infer that awareness generates or qualifies the awe; the packet does not state that causal order. `مَن يَخْشَىٰ` consequently reads as one whose fear contains recognition, not undirected alarm.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000413/B001","root_000413/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5323758d5f96e84492fe","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:10:3\",\"root_000413\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Korku, bilgisiz bir ürperti değil, söylenenin ne anlama geldiğini kavramanın doğurduğu bilinçli saygıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000413/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5bd5a2072b8703c81544","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:10:3\",\"root_000413\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kuruma çağrışımı, korkan kişinin otlaktan çerçöpe ve oradan ateşe uzanan kayıp sürecini önceden sezdiğini düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5f8736e3036f5838ebf9","text":"3. **Dryness/shriveling as the body-image behind receptivity.** Lexical evidence: خشي B004 shriveling/dryness; غثو B002 dried pasture into chaff in 87:5; زكو B001 growth/increase in 87:14. Structural cue: dried refuse precedes the fearful rememberer, and purification/growth follows. Assigned role: dryness is a possible negative bodily image that awe must pass through toward purification. Abductive move: the packet supplies dry/chaff imagery; I infer a fragile condition receptive to rescue. Reading change: the fearful person is not strong by himself; he is one who, aware of drying/perishing, can be recalled to life.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_63be60891e5b68bb5e43","text":"1. **Recollection is the receiving link in a transmission chain.** `ذ ك ر/B003` supplies bringing to mind as the opposite of forgetting. Within the window, 87:6 protects recitation from `ن س ي`, 87:9 issues and names the reminder, and 87:15 turns remembrance into mention of the Lord’s name and prayer. The fixed future verb receives what the earlier causative recitation and imperative set in motion. I infer one relay across these distinct subjects and forms. The ayah reads as the inward uptake between delivered speech and enacted response.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000413/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_774c5f70abdfd8692979","text":"2. **Knowing through fear.** Lexical evidence: خ ش ي B002 (knowing as figurative use), ع ل م B001 in 87:7, ج ه ر/خ ف ي in 87:7. Structural cue: divine knowledge of hidden/open precedes the human who \"fears/knows.\" Assigned role: khashya becomes knowledge of one's hidden exposure before God. Abductive move: awe arises from recognizing what is known beyond public appearance. Reading change: the ayah can read \"the one who knows in awe will remember.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000413/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7a0c9cb752e1cc5fe718","text":"3. **Exploratory: awe carries a material warning of desiccation.** `خ ش ي/B004` gives shriveling and dryness. At the back edge of the focus window, 87:5 has fresh pasture reduced to dry refuse; immediately ahead, 87:12 introduces fire. The dryness branch makes the fixed root a bodily/material anticipation of the route from flourishing to heat. I infer an analogy between perceived desiccation and caution; there is no explicit claim that the fearful person dries up. This weak but visible link lets recollection read as a preemptive response to a recognized process of loss.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7a648dfb15f246077faa","text":"1. **Awe unlocks remembrance.** Lexical evidence: ذكر B003 bringing to mind; خشي B001 fear/dread/reverent awe. Structural cue: the subject of future remembrance is defined only by خشية. Assigned role: خشية is the receptive condition; ذكر is the activated memory. Abductive move: the packet supplies fear and recall; I infer that affective awe opens the benefit named in 87:9. Reading change: remembrance is not a neutral cognitive event but an awe-shaped response.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8213a7a18b676f15d311","text":"87:11 made the receptive profile sharper through contrast with يتجنبها. 87:14 also surprised the ayah: the one who fears and remembers anticipates the one who تزكى, so خشية is not the end-state but the gateway to purification.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000413/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9b5d47e124ddcb945641","text":"87:15 completed an unexpectedly long relay: remembered recitation becomes voiced mention of the Lord’s name and then prayer. Before that, 87:12’s fire activated the weak material branch `خ ش ي/B004`, making the earlier dried pasture image read as a cautionary anticipation rather than an isolated ecological detail.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9bccbd99457734d8bd33","text":"4. **Future certainty of response.** Lexical evidence: ذكر B009 reminder; نفع B001 benefit. Structural cue: 87:9's conditional is answered by 87:10's future verb. Assigned role: the ayah names the successful case of the reminder. Abductive move: the packet supplies conditional benefit and future remembrance; I infer that the reminder's benefit is not lost, only targeted. Reading change: the ayah reassures the command: there will be a hearer in whom it works.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b81b9f8a1f552dce92b2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:10:1\",\"root_000516\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hatırlama, korunmuş okuma ile daha sonra Allah'ın adını anıp namaz kılma arasında içsel kabul halkasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b99bd7812d095ceb1b66","text":"87:5 made the dryness branch of خ ش ي visible; the fearful one is not just frightened but avoids the dried-out trajectory. 87:7 also made khashya knowledge-loaded, because divine knowledge of hiddenness is the context of human awe.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
